## Overview

Dr. Gary Steinberg is a neurosurgeon in Palo Alto, California and is affiliated with multiple hospitals in the area, including [Lucile Packard Children's Hospital Stanford](https://health.usnews.com/best-hospitals/area/ca/lucile-salter-packard-childrens-hospital-at-stanford-6933605) and [Palo Alto Health Care Medical Center](https://health.usnews.com/best-hospitals/area/ca/va-palo-alto-hospital-6930064). He received his medical degree from Stanford University School of Medicine and has been in practice for more than 20 years. Dr. Gary Steinberg has expertise in treating spinal stenosis and spondylosis, among other conditions - see all areas of [expertise](https://health.usnews.com/doctors/gary-steinberg-24094#expertise). Dr. Gary Steinberg accepts Aetna, Blue Cross, United Healthcare - see other [insurance plans accepted](https://health.usnews.com/doctors/gary-steinberg-24094#insurance).

Doctor's Details

## Specialties

Specialty

### Neurosurgery

Neurosurgeons use minimally invasive surgery to treat brain, nervous system and spinal nerve disorders. Conditions treated by neurosurgeons include brain bleeds, head or spinal cord trauma or tumors.

Subspecialties

### General Neurosurgery

## Frequently Asked Questions About Dr. Steinberg

### Can I make an appointment online with Dr. Steinberg?

Yes, you can [book an appointment](https://health.usnews.com/doctors/gary-steinberg-24094) with Dr. Steinberg online today. It's simple, secure, and free.

### Does Dr. Steinberg offer telehealth?

Call [Dr. Steinberg](https://health.usnews.com/doctors/gary-steinberg-24094) for more information on telehealth services.

### What do patients say about Dr. Steinberg?

We’ve published patient experience ratings for Dr. Steinberg in 6 key categories based on 18 reviews. [See ratings](https://health.usnews.com/doctors/gary-steinberg-24094#patient-experience).

### Dr. Steinberg accepts insurance

Check Dr. Steinberg's [profile](https://health.usnews.com/doctors/gary-steinberg-24094#insurance) to see which insurance they accept.

### Where is Dr. Steinberg's  office located?

Dr. Steinberg's office is located at
300 Pasteur Dr, Palo Alto, CA.
[View the map](https://health.usnews.com/doctors/gary-steinberg-24094#location).

### What are Dr. Steinberg's  areas of care?

Neurosurgeons use minimally invasive surgery to treat brain, nervous system and spinal nerve disorders. Conditions treated by neurosurgeons include brain bleeds, head or spinal cord trauma or tumors.
See all conditions on Dr. Steinberg's
[profile](https://health.usnews.com/doctors/gary-steinberg-24094).
